- **no-assumptions**: Use when the user invokes /no-assumptions. Forces the user to make every execution-critical assumption explicit before the model answers, so there is no missing implementation detail, no hidden constraint, and no room for the model to drift into guessing. Produces a refusal checklist of concrete questions and blocks all help until the request is precise enough to execute.
